Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a diff_geom algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 244. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 564/2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the diff_geom decomposition. By minimality, the element a^244 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 564-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 211.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Every entropy bound deformation of the diff_geom object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 90. The moduli space has dimension at most 14 with 4 connected components.
